\\\"# Sparsity predictor architecture: bottleneck MLP + Gumbel-sigmoid/STE mask + model wrapper.\\\\n# Compensation network: per-layer MLP correcting sparse MLP output errors (FastForward-style).\\\\n# Input: hidden_states (batch, seq, hidden_size) -> Output: mask logits (batch, seq, intermediate_size)\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass SparsityPredictor(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Rank-bottleneck MLP predicting SwiGLU sparsity masks.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, intermediate_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 128):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.down = nn.Linear(hidden_size, bottleneck_size)\\\\n        self.act = nn.GELU()\\\\n        self.up = nn.Linear(bottleneck_size, intermediate_size)\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        return self.up(self.act(self.down(hidden_states)))\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass CompensationHead(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Per-layer MLP predicting the output error introduced by sparsification.\\\\n\\\\n    Takes MLP input hidden state, outputs correction added to sparse MLP output.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 256):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_size, bottleneck_size),\\\\n            nn.GELU(),\\\\n            nn.Linear(bottleneck_size, hidden_size),\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        return self.net(hidden_states)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass CompensationNetwork(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Per-layer compensation heads correcting sparse MLP output errors.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, num_layers: int, hidden_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 256):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleList([\\\\n            CompensationHead(hidden_size, bottleneck_size)\\\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\\\n        ])\\\\n\\\\n    def forward_layer(self, layer_idx: int, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        return self.heads[layer_idx](hidden_states)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass GumbelSigmoidMask(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Differentiable binary mask via Gumbel-sigmoid relaxation.\\\\n\\\\n    Training: soft mask with Gumbel noise and temperature annealing.\\\\n    Inference: hard threshold (sigmoid(logits) > 0.5 <=> logits > 0).\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, tau: float = 1.0, hard: bool = False):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.tau = tau\\\\n        self.hard = hard\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        if self.training and not self.hard:\\\\n            u = torch.rand_like(logits).clamp(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)\\\\n            gumbel_noise = -torch.log(-torch.log(u))\\\\n            y_soft = torch.sigmoid((logits + gumbel_noise) / self.tau)\\\\n            return y_soft\\\\n        else:\\\\n            return (logits > 0).to(logits.dtype)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass STEMask(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Straight-Through Estimator binary mask.\\\\n\\\\n    Forward: hard threshold (sigmoid > 0.5).\\\\n    Backward: gradient flows through sigmoid (vanilla STE).\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        if self.training:\\\\n            sigmoid_out = torch.sigmoid(logits)\\\\n            hard = (sigmoid_out > 0.5).to(logits.dtype)\\\\n            return hard - sigmoid_out.detach() + sigmoid_out\\\\n        else:\\\\n            return (logits > 0).to(logits.dtype)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass PredictorWrapper(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Wraps a frozen LlamaForCausalLM with per-layer sparsity predictors.\\\\n\\\\n    Monkey-patches each MLP to optionally apply a predicted mask on the\\\\n    SwiGLU intermediate activation (act(gate_proj(x)) * up_proj(x)) before down_proj.\\\\n\\\\n    Modes:\\\\n        sparse_mode=True:  apply Gumbel-sigmoid mask (training/eval)\\\\n        sparse_mode=False: passthrough; optionally capture intermediates for BCE\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, model, bottleneck_size: int = 128):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.model = model\\\\n        for p in model.parameters():\\\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\\\n\\\\n        config = model.config\\\\n        num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\\\\n        hidden_size = config.hidden_size\\\\n        intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size\\\\n\\\\n        self.predictors = nn.ModuleList([\\\\n            SparsityPredictor(hidden_size, intermediate_size, bottleneck_size)\\\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\\\n        ])\\\\n        self.gumbel_mask = GumbelSigmoidMask()\\\\n\\\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\\\n        self.capture_intermediates = False\\\\n        self._layer_masks = {}\\\\n        self._layer_intermediates = {}\\\\n        self._layer_inputs = {}\\\\n\\\\n        # Compensation network (set externally, None by default)\\\\n        self.comp_network = None\\\\n        self.use_compensation = False\\\\n\\\\n        self._patch_mlps()\\\\n\\\\n    def _patch_mlps(self):\\\\n        for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(self.model.model.layers):\\\\n            self._patch_single_mlp(layer_idx, layer.mlp)\\\\n\\\\n    def _patch_single_mlp(self, layer_idx, mlp):\\\\n        gate_proj = mlp.gate_proj\\\\n        up_proj = mlp.up_proj\\\\n        down_proj = mlp.down_proj\\\\n        act_fn = mlp.act_fn\\\\n        wrapper = self\\\\n        predictor = self.predictors[layer_idx]\\\\n\\\\n        def patched_forward(x):\\\\n            gate = act_fn(gate_proj(x))\\\\n            up = up_proj(x)\\\\n            intermediate = gate * up\\\\n\\\\n            if wrapper.sparse_mode:\\\\n                logits = predictor(x)\\\\n                mask = wrapper.gumbel_mask(logits)\\\\n                wrapper._layer_masks[layer_idx] = mask\\\\n                result = down_proj(intermediate * mask)\\\\n                if wrapper.use_compensation and wrapper.comp_network is not None:\\\\n                    result = result + wrapper.comp_network.forward_layer(layer_idx, x)\\\\n                return result\\\\n            elif wrapper.capture_intermediates:\\\\n                wrapper._layer_intermediates[layer_idx] = intermediate.detach()\\\\n                wrapper._layer_inputs[layer_idx] = x.detach()\\\\n\\\\n            return down_proj(intermediate)\\\\n\\\\n        mlp.forward = patched_forward\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):\\\\n        self._layer_masks.clear()\\\\n        return self.model(\\\\n            input_ids=input_ids,\\\\n            attention_mask=attention_mask,\\\\n            labels=labels,\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def forward_dense(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, capture_intermediates=False):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Forward without masks. Optionally captures per-layer intermediates & inputs.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        self.sparse_mode = False\\\\n        self.capture_intermediates = capture_intermediates\\\\n        self._layer_intermediates.clear()\\\\n        self._layer_inputs.clear()\\\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\\\n            outputs = self.model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\\\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\\\n        self.capture_intermediates = False\\\\n        return outputs\\\\n\\\\n    def forward_sparse(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Forward with predicted sparsity masks.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\\\n        return self.forward(input_ids, attention_mask, labels)\\\\n\\\\n    def get_masks(self):\\\\n        return dict(self._layer_masks)\\\\n\\\\n    def get_intermediates(self):\\\\n        return dict(self._layer_intermediates)\\\\n\\\\n    def get_layer_inputs(self):\\\\n        return dict(self._layer_inputs)\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.43}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Sparsity predictor architecture: bottleneck MLP + Gumbel-sigmoid/STE mask + model wrapper.\\n# Compensation network: per-layer MLP correcting sparse MLP output errors (FastForward-style).\\n# Input: hidden_states (batch, seq, hidden_size) -> Output: mask logits (batch, seq, intermediate_size)\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\n\\n\\nclass SparsityPredictor(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Rank-bottleneck MLP predicting SwiGLU sparsity masks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, intermediate_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 128):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.down = nn.Linear(hidden_size, bottleneck_size)\\n        self.act = nn.GELU()\\n        self.up = nn.Linear(bottleneck_size, intermediate_size)\\n\\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.up(self.act(self.down(hidden_states)))\\n\\n\\nclass CompensationHead(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Per-layer MLP predicting the output error introduced by sparsification.\\n\\n    Takes MLP input hidden state, outputs correction added to sparse MLP output.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 256):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_size, bottleneck_size),\\n            nn.GELU(),\\n            nn.Linear(bottleneck_size, hidden_size),\\n        )\\n\\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.net(hidden_states)\\n\\n\\nclass CompensationNetwork(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Per-layer compensation heads correcting sparse MLP output errors.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, num_layers: int, hidden_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 256):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleList([\\n            CompensationHead(hidden_size, bottleneck_size)\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\n        ])\\n\\n    def forward_layer(self, layer_idx: int, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.heads[layer_idx](hidden_states)\\n\\n\\nclass GumbelSigmoidMask(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Differentiable binary mask via Gumbel-sigmoid relaxation.\\n\\n    Training: soft mask with Gumbel noise and temperature annealing.\\n    Inference: hard threshold (sigmoid(logits) > 0.5 <=> logits > 0).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, tau: float = 1.0, hard: bool = False):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.tau = tau\\n        self.hard = hard\\n\\n    def forward(self, logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        if self.training and not self.hard:\\n            u = torch.rand_like(logits).clamp(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)\\n            gumbel_noise = -torch.log(-torch.log(u))\\n            y_soft = torch.sigmoid((logits + gumbel_noise) / self.tau)\\n            return y_soft\\n        else:\\n            return (logits > 0).to(logits.dtype)\\n\\n\\n\\nclass STEMask(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Straight-Through Estimator binary mask.\\n\\n    Forward: hard threshold (sigmoid > 0.5).\\n    Backward: gradient flows through sigmoid (vanilla STE).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def forward(self, logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        if self.training:\\n            sigmoid_out = torch.sigmoid(logits)\\n            hard = (sigmoid_out > 0.5).to(logits.dtype)\\n            return hard - sigmoid_out.detach() + sigmoid_out\\n        else:\\n            return (logits > 0).to(logits.dtype)\\n\\n\\n\\nclass PredictorWrapper(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Wraps a frozen LlamaForCausalLM with per-layer sparsity predictors.\\n\\n    Monkey-patches each MLP to optionally apply a predicted mask on the\\n    SwiGLU intermediate activation (act(gate_proj(x)) * up_proj(x)) before down_proj.\\n\\n    Modes:\\n        sparse_mode=True:  apply Gumbel-sigmoid mask (training/eval)\\n        sparse_mode=False: passthrough; optionally capture intermediates for BCE\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, model, bottleneck_size: int = 128):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.model = model\\n        for p in model.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n\\n        config = model.config\\n        num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\\n        hidden_size = config.hidden_size\\n        intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size\\n\\n        self.predictors = nn.ModuleList([\\n            SparsityPredictor(hidden_size, intermediate_size, bottleneck_size)\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\n        ])\\n        self.gumbel_mask = GumbelSigmoidMask()\\n\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        self.capture_intermediates = False\\n        self._layer_masks = {}\\n        self._layer_intermediates = {}\\n        self._layer_inputs = {}\\n\\n        # Compensation network (set externally, None by default)\\n        self.comp_network = None\\n        self.use_compensation = False\\n\\n        self._patch_mlps()\\n\\n    def _patch_mlps(self):\\n        for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(self.model.model.layers):\\n            self._patch_single_mlp(layer_idx, layer.mlp)\\n\\n    def _patch_single_mlp(self, layer_idx, mlp):\\n        gate_proj = mlp.gate_proj\\n        up_proj = mlp.up_proj\\n        down_proj = mlp.down_proj\\n        act_fn = mlp.act_fn\\n        wrapper = self\\n        predictor = self.predictors[layer_idx]\\n\\n        def patched_forward(x):\\n            gate = act_fn(gate_proj(x))\\n            up = up_proj(x)\\n            intermediate = gate * up\\n\\n            if wrapper.sparse_mode:\\n                logits = predictor(x)\\n                mask = wrapper.gumbel_mask(logits)\\n                wrapper._layer_masks[layer_idx] = mask\\n                result = down_proj(intermediate * mask)\\n                if wrapper.use_compensation and wrapper.comp_network is not None:\\n                    result = result + wrapper.comp_network.forward_layer(layer_idx, x)\\n                return result\\n            elif wrapper.capture_intermediates:\\n                wrapper._layer_intermediates[layer_idx] = intermediate.detach()\\n                wrapper._layer_inputs[layer_idx] = x.detach()\\n\\n            return down_proj(intermediate)\\n\\n        mlp.forward = patched_forward\\n\\n    def forward(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):\\n        self._layer_masks.clear()\\n        return self.model(\\n            input_ids=input_ids,\\n            attention_mask=attention_mask,\\n            labels=labels,\\n        )\\n\\n    def forward_dense(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, capture_intermediates=False):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Forward without masks. Optionally captures per-layer intermediates & inputs.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.sparse_mode = False\\n        self.capture_intermediates = capture_intermediates\\n        self._layer_intermediates.clear()\\n        self._layer_inputs.clear()\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = self.model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        self.capture_intermediates = False\\n        return outputs\\n\\n    def forward_sparse(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Forward with predicted sparsity masks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        return self.forward(input_ids, attention_mask, labels)\\n\\n    def get_masks(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_masks)\\n\\n    def get_intermediates(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_intermediates)\\n\\n    def get_layer_inputs(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_inputs)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}}}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016qqsphKeDk8QLaFJfDnF3J","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"# Loss functions: KL distillation, BCE activation prediction, compensation MSE, sparsity regularizer.\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass KLDistillLoss(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"KL(P_dense || P_sparse) on output logit distributions.\\\\n\\\\n    P = softmax(logits / T). Scaled by T^2 so gradient magnitude is\\\\n    independent of temperature.\\\\n    Normalized to per-token-per-dim scale.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, temperature: float = 1.0):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.temperature = temperature\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, dense_logits: torch.Tensor, sparse_logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        p = F.softmax(dense_logits / self.temperature, dim=-1)\\\\n        log_q = F.log_softmax(sparse_logits / self.temperature, dim=-1)\\\\n        kl = F.kl_div(log_q, p, reduction=\\\\\\\"batchmean\\\\\\\") * (self.temperature ** 2)\\\\n        seq_len = dense_logits.shape[-2]\\\\n        return kl / seq_len\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass BCESparsityLoss(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"BCE between predictor logits and top-k binary activation targets.\\\\n\\\\n    For each position, the top (1-sparsity) fraction of neurons by absolute\\\\n    activation value are labeled 1 (keep); the rest 0 (prune).\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, sparsity_target: float = 0.5):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.sparsity_target = sparsity_target\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, predictor_logits: torch.Tensor, dense_intermediate: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        k = int(dense_intermediate.shape[-1] * (1 - self.sparsity_target))\\\\n        abs_vals = dense_intermediate.abs()\\\\n        topk_vals, _ = abs_vals.topk(k, dim=-1)\\\\n        threshold = topk_vals[..., -1:]\\\\n        binary_target = (abs_vals >= threshold).float()\\\\n        return F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(predictor_logits, binary_target)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass CompensationLoss(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"MSE between (sparse_mlp_output + compensation) and dense_mlp_output.\\\\n\\\\n    Operates per-layer in hidden_size space (after down_proj).\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, compensation: torch.Tensor, sparse_output: torch.Tensor,\\\\n                dense_output: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        return F.mse_loss(sparse_output + compensation, dense_output)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass SparsityRegularizer(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"L1 penalty driving average mask density toward (1 - target_sparsity).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, target_sparsity: float = 0.5, weight: float = 1.0):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.target_sparsity = target_sparsity\\\\n        self.weight = weight\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, masks: dict) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        if not masks:\\\\n            return torch.tensor(0.0)\\\\n        penalties = []\\\\n        for mask in masks.values():\\\\n            density = mask.mean()\\\\n            penalties.append(torch.abs(density - (1.0 - self.target_sparsity)))\\\\n        return self.weight * torch.stack(penalties).mean()\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.51}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73c3b854-fd2a-4cd9-baf1-2aece9e3a5f6","uuid":"ead24015-384f-4fb4-b86c-8d46d57ba835","timestamp":"2026-05-17T04:03:42.282Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"# Loss functions: KL distillation, BCE activation prediction, compensation MSE, sparsity regularizer.\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass KLDistillLoss(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"KL(P_dense || P_sparse) on output logit distributions.\\\\n\\\\n    P = softmax(logits / T). target_sparsity).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, target_sparsity: float = 0.5, weight: float = 1.0):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.target_sparsity = target_sparsity\\\\n        self.weight = weight\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, masks: dict) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        if not masks:\\\\n            return torch.tensor(0.0)\\\\n        penalties = []\\\\n        for mask in masks.values():\\\\n            density = mask.mean()\\\\n            penalties.append(torch.abs(density - (1.0 - self.target_sparsity)))\\\\n        return self.weight * torch.stack(penalties).mean()\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.51}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Loss functions: KL distillation, BCE activation prediction, compensation MSE, sparsity regularizer.\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\n\\n\\nclass KLDistillLoss(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"KL(P_dense || P_sparse) on output logit distributions.\\n\\n    P = softmax(logits / T). Scaled by T^2 so gradient magnitude is\\n    independent of temperature.\\n    Normalized to per-token-per-dim scale.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, temperature: float = 1.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.temperature = temperature\\n\\n    def forward(self, dense_logits: torch.Tensor, sparse_logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        p = F.softmax(dense_logits / self.temperature, dim=-1)\\n        log_q = F.log_softmax(sparse_logits / self.temperature, dim=-1)\\n        kl = F.kl_div(log_q, p, reduction=\\\"batchmean\\\") * (self.temperature ** 2)\\n        seq_len = dense_logits.shape[-2]\\n        return kl / seq_len\\n\\n\\nclass BCESparsityLoss(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"BCE between predictor logits and top-k binary activation targets.\\n\\n    For each position, the top (1-sparsity) fraction of neurons by absolute\\n    activation value are labeled 1 (keep); the rest 0 (prune).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, sparsity_target: float = 0.5):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.sparsity_target = sparsity_target\\n\\n    def forward(self, predictor_logits: torch.Tensor, dense_intermediate: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        k = int(dense_intermediate.shape[-1] * (1 - self.sparsity_target))\\n        abs_vals = dense_intermediate.abs()\\n        topk_vals, _ = abs_vals.topk(k, dim=-1)\\n        threshold = topk_vals[..., -1:]\\n        binary_target = (abs_vals >= threshold).float()\\n        return F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(predictor_logits, binary_target)\\n\\n\\nclass CompensationLoss(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"MSE between (sparse_mlp_output + compensation) and dense_mlp_output.\\n\\n    Operates per-layer in hidden_size space (after down_proj).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def forward(self, compensation: torch.Tensor, sparse_output: torch.Tensor,\\n                dense_output: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return F.mse_loss(sparse_output + compensation, dense_output)\\n\\n\\nclass SparsityRegularizer(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"L1 penalty driving average mask density toward (1 - target_sparsity).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, target_sparsity: float = 0.5, weight: float = 1.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.target_sparsity = target_sparsity\\n        self.weight = weight\\n\\n    def forward(self, masks: dict) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        if not masks:\\n            return torch.tensor(0.0)\\n        penalties = []\\n        for mask in masks.values():\\n            density = mask.mean()\\n            penalties.append(torch.abs(density - (1.0 - self.target_sparsity)))\\n        return self.weight * torch.stack(penalties).mean()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.51}}"}}}

\\\"def train_kl(wrapper, dataloader, args, device):\\\\n    kl_fn = KLDistillLoss(temperature=args.kl_temperature)\\\\n\\\\n    params = list(wrapper.predictors.parameters())\\\\n    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=args.lr, weight_decay=0.01)\\\\n    scheduler = cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, args.warmup_steps, args.num_steps)\\\\n\\\\n    wrapper.predictors.train()\\\\n\\\\n    if args.gradient_checkpointing:\\\\n        wrapper.model.train()\\\\n        wrapper.model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(\\\\n            gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={\\\\\\\"use_reentrant\\\\\\\": False}\\\\n        )\\\\n        print(\\\\\\\"Gradient checkpointing enabled\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    data_iter = iter(dataloader)\\\\n    ga = args.gradient_accumulation_steps\\\\n\\\\n    lambda_sparse = 1.0\\\\n    target_sparsity = args.sparsity_target\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"Lagrangian mode: lambda_sparse={lambda_sparse:.2f}, target_sparsity={target_sparsity}, update_every=25, tol=0.02, cap={args.lambda_max}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    for opt_step in range(1, args.num_steps + 1):\\\\n        if args.relaxation != \\\\\\\"ste\\\\\\\":\\\\n            tau = args.tau_start + (args.tau_end - args.tau_start) * (opt_step / args.num_steps)\\\\n            wrapper.gumbel_mask.tau = tau\\\\n        else:\\\\n            tau = 0.0\\\\n\\\\n        accum_loss = 0.0\\\\n        accum_kl = 0.0\\\\n        accum_constraint = 0.0\\\\n\\\\n        for _ in range(ga):\\\\n            try:\\\\n                batch = next(data_iter)\\\\n            except StopIteration:\\\\n                data_iter = iter(dataloader)\\\\n                batch = next(data_iter)\\\\n\\\\n            input_ids = batch[\\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\"].to(device)\\\\n            dense_logits = wrapper.forward_dense(input_ids).logits.detach()\\\\n            sparse_logits = wrapper.forward_sparse(input_ids).logits\\\\n\\\\n            kl = kl_fn(dense_logits, sparse_logits)\\\\n\\\\n            if opt_step == 1:\\\\n                with torch.no_grad():\\\\n                    sl, vs = dense_logits.shape[-2], dense_logits.shape[-1]\\\\n                    print(f\\\\\\\"  [Diag] KL normalized: {kl.item():.6f}, raw batchmean would be: {kl.item() * sl * vs:.4f}, seq_len={sl}, vocab_size={vs}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n            masks = wrapper.get_masks()\\\\n            actual_sparsity = 1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in masks.values()]).mean()\\\\n            sparsity_violation = target_sparsity - actual_sparsity\\\\n            constraint_loss = lambda_sparse * torch.clamp(sparsity_violation, min=0.0)\\\\n\\\\n            loss = (kl + constraint_loss) / ga\\\\n            loss.backward()\\\\n\\\\n            accum_loss += loss.item()\\\\n            accum_kl += kl.item() / ga\\\\n            accum_constraint += constraint_loss.item() / ga\\\\n\\\\n        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)\\\\n        optimizer.step()\\\\n        scheduler.step()\\\\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\\\\n\\\\n        masks = wrapper.get_masks()\\\\n        avg_sparsity = (\\\\n            1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in masks.values()]).mean().item()\\\\n            if masks else 0.0\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        if opt_step % 25 == 0 and opt_step > 0:\\\\n            old_lambda = lambda_sparse\\\\n            if avg_sparsity < target_sparsity - 0.02:\\\\n                lambda_sparse *= 2.0\\\\n            elif avg_sparsity > target_sparsity + 0.02:\\\\n                lambda_sparse *= 0.5\\\\n            lambda_sparse = max(0.1, min(lambda_sparse, args.lambda_max))\\\\n            if old_lambda != lambda_sparse:\\\\n                print(f\\\\\\\"  [Lambda update] step={opt_step} sparsity={avg_sparsity:.3f} lambda: {old_lambda:.2f} -> {lambda_sparse:.2f}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        log_dict = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"loss\\\\\\\": accum_loss, \\\\\\\"kl_loss\\\\\\\": accum_kl, \\\\\\\"constraint_loss\\\\\\\": accum_constraint,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"sparsity\\\\\\\": avg_sparsity, \\\\\\\"tau\\\\\\\": tau, \\\\\\\"lr\\\\\\\": scheduler.get_last_lr()[0],\\\\n            \\\\\\\"lambda_sparse\\\\\\\": lambda_sparse,\\\\n        }\\\\n        wandb.log(log_dict, step=opt_step)\\\\n\\\\n        if f\\\\\\\"tau={tau:.3f} lr={scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e}\\\\\\\"\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": KLDistillLoss(temperature=args.kl_temperature)\\n\\n    params = list(wrapper.predictors.parameters())\\n    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=args.lr, weight_decay=0.01)\\n    scheduler = cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, args.warmup_steps, args.num_steps)\\n\\n    wrapper.predictors.train()\\n\\n    if args.gradient_checkpointing:\\n        wrapper.model.train()\\n        wrapper.model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(\\n            gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={\\\"use_reentrant\\\": False}\\n        )\\n        print(\\\"Gradient checkpointing enabled\\\")\\n\\n    data_iter = iter(dataloader)\\n    ga = args.gradient_accumulation_steps\\n\\n    lambda_sparse = 1.0\\n    target_sparsity = args.sparsity_target\\n    print(f\\\"Lagrangian mode: lambda_sparse={lambda_sparse:.2f}, target_sparsity={target_sparsity}, update_every=25, tol=0.02, cap={args.lambda_max}\\\")\\n\\n    for opt_step in range(1, args.num_steps + 1):\\n        if args.relaxation != \\\"ste\\\":\\n            tau = args.tau_start + (args.tau_end - args.tau_start) * (opt_step / args.num_steps)\\n            wrapper.gumbel_mask.tau = tau\\n        else:\\n            tau = 0.0\\n\\n        accum_loss = 0.0\\n        accum_kl = 0.0\\n        accum_constraint = 0.0\\n\\n        for _ in range(ga):\\n            try:\\n                batch = next(data_iter)\\n            except StopIteration:\\n                data_iter = iter(dataloader)\\n                batch = next(data_iter)\\n\\n            input_ids = batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"].to(device)\\n            dense_logits = wrapper.forward_dense(input_ids).logits.detach()\\n            sparse_logits = wrapper.forward_sparse(input_ids).logits\\n\\n            kl = kl_fn(dense_logits, sparse_logits)\\n\\n            if opt_step == 1:\\n                with torch.no_grad():\\n                    sl, vs = dense_logits.shape[-2], dense_logits.shape[-1]\\n                    print(f\\\"  [Diag] KL normalized: {kl.item():.6f}, raw batchmean would be: {kl.item() * sl * vs:.4f}, seq_len={sl}, vocab_size={vs}\\\")\\n\\n            masks = wrapper.get_masks()\\n            actual_sparsity = 1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in masks.values()]).mean()\\n            sparsity_violation = target_sparsity - actual_sparsity\\n            constraint_loss = lambda_sparse * torch.clamp(sparsity_violation, min=0.0)\\n\\n            loss = (kl + constraint_loss) / ga\\n            loss.backward()\\n\\n            accum_loss += loss.item()\\n            accum_kl += kl.item() / ga\\n            accum_constraint += constraint_loss.item() / ga\\n\\n        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)\\n        optimizer.step()\\n        scheduler.step()\\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\\n\\n        masks = wrapper.get_masks()\\n        avg_sparsity = (\\n            1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in masks.values()]).mean().item()\\n            if masks else 0.0\\n        )\\n\\n        if opt_step % 25 == 0 and opt_step > 0:\\n            old_lambda = lambda_sparse\\n            if avg_sparsity < target_sparsity - 0.02:\\n                lambda_sparse *= 2.0\\n            elif avg_sparsity > target_sparsity + 0.02:\\n                lambda_sparse *= 0.5\\n            lambda_sparse = max(0.1, min(lambda_sparse, args.lambda_max))\\n            if old_lambda != lambda_sparse:\\n                print(f\\\"  [Lambda update] step={opt_step} sparsity={avg_sparsity:.3f} lambda: {old_lambda:.2f} -> {lambda_sparse:.2f}\\\")\\n\\n        log_dict = {\\n            \\\"loss\\\": accum_loss, \\\"kl_loss\\\": accum_kl, \\\"constraint_loss\\\": accum_constraint,\\n            \\\"sparsity\\\": avg_sparsity, \\\"tau\\\": tau, \\\"lr\\\": scheduler.get_last_lr()[0],\\n            \\\"lambda_sparse\\\": lambda_sparse,\\n        }\\n        wandb.log(log_dict, step=opt_step)\\n\\n        if opt_step % 10 == 0 or opt_step == 1:\\n            print(\\n                f\\\"[Step {opt_step}/{args.num_steps}] loss={accum_loss:.4f} \\\"\\n                f\\\"kl={accum_kl:.4f} constraint={accum_constraint:.4f} \\\"\\n                f\\\"sparsity={avg_sparsity:.3f} lambda={lambda_sparse:.2f} \\\"\\n                f\\\"tau={tau:.3f} lr={scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e}\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.5}}"}}}

\\\"def evaluate_perplexity(model, examples, device, global_masks=None):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Compute perplexity with optional fixed masks on SwiGLU intermediates.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    original_forwards = {}\\\\n\\\\n    if global_masks is not None:\\\\n        for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(model.model.layers):\\\\n            if layer_idx not in global_masks:\\\\n                continue\\\\n            original_forwards[layer_idx] = layer.mlp.forward\\\\n            mask = global_masks[layer_idx].to(device=device,\\\\n                                               dtype=layer.mlp.gate_proj.weight.dtype)\\\\n            mlp = layer.mlp\\\\n\\\\n            def make_masked_fwd(gp, up, dp, af, m):\\\\n                def fwd(x):\\\\n                    intermediate = af(gp(x)) * up(x)\\\\n                    return dp(intermediate * m.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0))\\\\n                return fwd\\\\n\\\\n            layer.mlp.forward = make_masked_fwd(\\\\n                mlp.gate_proj, mlp.up_proj, mlp.down_proj, mlp.act_fn, mask\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n    total_loss = 0.0\\\\n    total_tokens = 0\\\\n    for ex in examples:\\\\n        ids = ex[\\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\"].unsqueeze(0).to(device)\\\\n        labels = ex[\\\\\\\"labels\\\\\\\"].unsqueeze(0).to(device)\\\\n        logits = model(input_ids=ids).logits\\\\n        loss = F.cross_entropy(\\\\n            logits.view(-1, logits.size(-1)), labels.view(-1), reduction=\\\\\\\"sum\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n        total_loss += loss.item()\\\\n        total_tokens += labels.numel()\\\\n\\\\n    for layer_idx, fwd in original_forwards.items():\\\\n        model.model.layers[layer_idx].mlp.forward = fwd\\\\n\\\\n    return torch.exp(torch.tensor(total_loss / total_tokens)).item()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@torch.no_grad()\\\\ndef evaluate_true_dense(model, examples, device):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Evaluate with original MLP forwards — no predictor, no mask.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    patched_forwards = {}\\\\n    for idx, layer in enumerate(model.model.layers):\\\\n        patched_forwards[idx] = layer.mlp.forward\\\\n        layer.mlp.forward = _make_original_fwd(layer.mlp)\\\\n\\\\n    ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, examples, device)\\\\n\\\\n    for idx, fwd in patched_forwards.items():\\\\n        model.model.layers[idx].mlp.forward = fwd\\\\n\\\\n    return ppl\\\\n\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017XwDyAprNVTGWRcJ9CzyvJ","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"def train_kl_normalized(wrapper, dataloader, args, device):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"KL with per-token-per-dim normalization + aggressive Lagrangian.\\\\n\\\\n    KL already per-token (batchmean / seq_len from KLDistillLoss). No further division.\\\\n    Soft constraint: clamp(|sparsity - target| - margin, 0)^2 for smooth gradients.\\\\n    Lambda: every-step update, x2.0 growth, x0.5 decay, cap={args.lambda_max}.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    kl_fn = KLDistillLoss(temperature=args.kl_temperature)\\\\n\\\\n    params = list(wrapper.predictors.parameters())\\\\n    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=args.lr, weight_decay=0.01)\\\\n    scheduler = cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, args.warmup_steps, args.num_steps)\\\\n\\\\n    wrapper.predictors.train()\\\\n\\\\n    if args.gradient_checkpointing:\\\\n        wrapper.model.train()\\\\n        wrapper.model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(\\\\n            gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={\\\\\\\"use_reentrant\\\\\\\": False}\\\\n        )\\\\n        print(\\\\\\\"Gradient checkpointing enabled\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    data_iter = iter(dataloader)\\\\n    ga = args.gradient_accumulation_steps\\\\n\\\\n    lambda_sparse = 1.0\\\\n    target_sparsity = args.sparsity_target\\\\n    margin = 0.02\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"KL-normalized Lagrangian: lambda={lambda_sparse}, target={target_sparsity}, \\\\\\\"\\\\n          f\\\\\\\"margin={margin}, update=every_step, growth=2.0x, decay=0.5x, cap={args.lambda_max}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    for opt_step in range(1, args.num_steps + 1):\\\\n        if args.relaxation != \\\\\\\"ste\\\\\\\":\\\\n            tau = args.tau_start + (args.tau_end - args.tau_start) * (opt_step / args.num_steps)\\\\n            wrapper.gumbel_mask.tau = tau\\\\n        else:\\\\n            tau = 0.0\\\\n\\\\n        accum_loss = 0.0\\\\n        accum_kl_raw = 0.0\\\\n        accum_kl_norm = 0.0\\\\n        accum_constraint = 0.0\\\\n\\\\n        for _ in range(ga):\\\\n            try:\\\\n                batch = next(data_iter)\\\\n            except StopIteration:\\\\n                data_iter = iter(dataloader)\\\\n                batch = next(data_iter)\\\\n\\\\n            input_ids = batch[\\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\"].to(device)\\\\n            dense_logits = wrapper.forward_dense(input_ids).logits.detach()\\\\n            sparse_logits = wrapper.forward_sparse(input_ids).logits\\\\n\\\\n            # kl_fn returns per-token KL (batchmean / seq_len), summed over vocab\\\\n            kl_raw = kl_fn(dense_logits, sparse_logits)\\\\n            kl_norm = kl_raw\\\\n\\\\n            masks = wrapper.get_masks()\\\\n            actual_sparsity = 1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in masks.values()]).mean()\\\\n\\\\n            # Soft constraint: clamp(|sparsity - target| - margin, 0)^2\\\\n            sparsity_error = torch.abs(actual_sparsity - target_sparsity)\\\\n            constraint_loss = lambda_sparse * torch.clamp(sparsity_error - margin, min=0.0) ** 2\\\\n\\\\n            loss = (kl_norm + constraint_loss) / ga\\\\n            loss.backward()\\\\n\\\\n            accum_loss += loss.item()\\\\n            accum_kl_raw += kl_raw.item() / ga\\\\n            accum_kl_norm += kl_norm.item() / ga\\\\n            accum_constraint += constraint_loss.item() / ga\\\\n\\\\n        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)\\\\n        optimizer.step()\\\\n        scheduler.step()\\\\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\\\\n\\\\n        masks = wrapper.get_masks()\\\\n        avg_sparsity = (\\\\n            1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in masks.values()]).mean().item()\\\\n            if masks else 0.0\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        # Aggressive Lagrangian: every-step update, x2.0/x0.5, margin=0.02, cap=lambda_max\\\\n        old_lambda = lambda_sparse\\\\n        if avg_sparsity < target_sparsity - margin:\\\\n            lambda_sparse *= 2.0\\\\n        elif avg_sparsity > target_sparsity + margin:\\\\n            lambda_sparse *= 0.5\\\\n        lambda_sparse = max(0.01, min(lambda_sparse, args.lambda_max))\\\\n\\\\n        log_dict = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"loss\\\\\\\": accum_loss, \\\\\\\"kl_raw\\\\\\\": accum_kl_raw, \\\\\\\"kl_normalized\\\\\\\": accum_kl_norm,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"constraint_loss\\\\\\\": accum_constraint, \\\\\\\"sparsity\\\\\\\": avg_sparsity,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"tau\\\\\\\": tau, \\\\\\\"lr\\\\\\\": scheduler.get_last_lr()[0], \\\\\\\"lambda_sparse\\\\\\\": lambda_sparse,\\\\n        }\\\\n        wandb.log(log_dict, step=opt_step)\\\\n\\\\n        if opt_step % 10 == 0 or opt_step == 1:\\\\n            print(\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"[Step {opt_step}/{args.num_steps}] loss={accum_loss:.6f} \\\\\\\"\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"kl_raw={accum_kl_raw:.4f} kl_norm={accum_kl_norm:.6f} \\\\\\\"\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"constraint={accum_constraint:.6f} sparsity={avg_sparsity:.3f} \\\\\\\"\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"lambda={lambda_sparse:.2f} tau={tau:.3f} lr={scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e}\\\\\\\"\\\\n            )\\\\n            if old_lambda != lambda_sparse:\\\\n                print(f\\\\\\\"  [Lambda] {old_lambda:.2f} -> {lambda_sparse:.2f}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        if args.save_every > 0 and (opt_step % args.save_every == 0 or opt_step == args.num_steps):\\\\n            ckpt_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, f\\\\\\\"predictor_{args.loss_type}_step_{opt_step}.pt\\\\\\\")\\\\n 0.43}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"def train_kl_normalized(wrapper, dataloader, args, device):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"KL with per-token-per-dim normalization + aggressive Lagrangian.\\n\\n    KL already per-token (batchmean / seq_len from KLDistillLoss). No further division.\\n    Soft constraint: clamp(|sparsity - target| - margin, 0)^2 for smooth gradients.\\n    Lambda: every-step update, x2.0 growth, x0.5 decay, cap={args.lambda_max}.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    kl_fn = KLDistillLoss(temperature=args.kl_temperature)\\n\\n    params = list(wrapper.predictors.parameters())\\n    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=args.lr, weight_decay=0.01)\\n    scheduler = cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, args.warmup_steps, args.num_steps)\\n\\n    wrapper.predictors.train()\\n\\n    if args.gradient_checkpointing:\\n        wrapper.model.train()\\n        wrapper.model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(\\n            gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={\\\"use_reentrant\\\": False}\\n        )\\n        print(\\\"Gradient checkpointing enabled\\\")\\n\\n    data_iter = iter(dataloader)\\n    ga = args.gradient_accumulation_steps\\n\\n    lambda_sparse = 1.0\\n    target_sparsity = args.sparsity_target\\n    margin = 0.02\\n    print(f\\\"KL-normalized Lagrangian: lambda={lambda_sparse}, target={target_sparsity}, \\\"\\n          f\\\"margin={margin}, update=every_step, growth=2.0x, decay=0.5x, cap={args.lambda_max}\\\")\\n\\n    for opt_step in range(1, args.num_steps + 1):\\n        if args.relaxation != \\\"ste\\\":\\n            tau = args.tau_start + (args.tau_end - args.tau_start) * (opt_step / args.num_steps)\\n            wrapper.gumbel_mask.tau = tau\\n        else:\\n            tau = 0.0\\n\\n        accum_loss = 0.0\\n        accum_kl_raw = 0.0\\n        accum_kl_norm = 0.0\\n        accum_constraint = 0.0\\n\\n        for _ in range(ga):\\n            try:\\n                batch = next(data_iter)\\n            except StopIteration:\\n                data_iter = iter(dataloader)\\n                batch = next(data_iter)\\n\\n            input_ids = batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"].to(device)\\n            dense_logits = wrapper.forward_dense(input_ids).logits.detach()\\n            sparse_logits = wrapper.forward_sparse(input_ids).logits\\n\\n            # kl_fn returns per-token KL (batchmean / seq_len), summed over vocab\\n            kl_raw = kl_fn(dense_logits, sparse_logits)\\n            kl_norm = kl_raw\\n\\n            masks = wrapper.get_masks()\\n            actual_sparsity = 1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in masks.values()]).mean()\\n\\n            # Soft constraint: clamp(|sparsity - target| - margin, 0)^2\\n            sparsity_error = torch.abs(actual_sparsity - target_sparsity)\\n            constraint_loss = lambda_sparse * torch.clamp(sparsity_error - margin, min=0.0) ** 2\\n\\n            loss = (kl_norm + constraint_loss) / ga\\n            loss.backward()\\n\\n            accum_loss += loss.item()\\n            accum_kl_raw += kl_raw.item() / ga\\n            accum_kl_norm += kl_norm.item() / ga\\n            accum_constraint += constraint_loss.item() / ga\\n\\n        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)\\n        optimizer.step()\\n        scheduler.step()\\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\\n\\n        masks = wrapper.get_masks()\\n        avg_sparsity = (\\n            1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in masks.values()]).mean().item()\\n            if masks else 0.0\\n        )\\n\\n        # Aggressive Lagrangian: every-step update, x2.0/x0.5, margin=0.02, cap=lambda_max\\n        old_lambda = lambda_sparse\\n        if avg_sparsity < target_sparsity - margin:\\n            lambda_sparse *= 2.0\\n        elif avg_sparsity > target_sparsity + margin:\\n            lambda_sparse *= 0.5\\n        lambda_sparse = max(0.01, min(lambda_sparse, args.lambda_max))\\n\\n        log_dict = {\\n            \\\"loss\\\": accum_loss, \\\"kl_raw\\\": accum_kl_raw, \\\"kl_normalized\\\": accum_kl_norm,\\n            \\\"constraint_loss\\\": accum_constraint, \\\"sparsity\\\": avg_sparsity,\\n            \\\"tau\\\": tau, \\\"lr\\\": scheduler.get_last_lr()[0], \\\"lambda_sparse\\\": lambda_sparse,\\n        }\\n        wandb.log(log_dict, step=opt_step)\\n\\n        if opt_step % 10 == 0 or opt_step == 1:\\n            print(\\n                f\\\"[Step {opt_step}/{args.num_steps}] loss={accum_loss:.6f} \\\"\\n                f\\\"kl_raw={accum_kl_raw:.4f} kl_norm={accum_kl_norm:.6f} \\\"\\n                f\\\"constraint={accum_constraint:.6f} sparsity={avg_sparsity:.3f} \\\"\\n                f\\\"lambda={lambda_sparse:.2f} tau={tau:.3f} lr={scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e}\\\"\\n            )\\n            if old_lambda != lambda_sparse:\\n                print(f\\\"  [Lambda] {old_lambda:.2f} -> {lambda_sparse:.2f}\\\")\\n\\n        if args.save_every > 0 and (opt_step % args.save_every == 0 or opt_step == args.num_steps):\\n            ckpt_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, f\\\"predictor_{args.loss_type}_step_{opt_step}.pt\\\")\\n            os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\\n            torch.save({\\\"predictors\\\": wrapper.predictors.state_dict(), \\\"args\\\": vars(args)}, ckpt_path)\\n            print(f\\\"Saved checkpoint -> {ckpt_path}\\\")\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01NbAQpCW4XxLUH5N3uQd5jm","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012aYRHpmjghMdyR4PGDFuXb","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-34887","command":"cat << 'EOF' > /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/scripts/train_kl_mag_reg.py\n# Magnitude-preservation regularization for KL sparsity predictor.\n# Adds penalty for pruning high-magnitude neurons: loss = KL + lagrangian + alpha * mean((1-mask) * importance)\n\nimport argparse\nimport importlib.util\nimport math\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport json\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\n\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), '..', 'src'))\nfrom data_utils import get_calibration_loader, get_eval_dataset\nfrom losses import KLDistillLoss\nfrom predictor import PredictorWrapper\n\n\ndef _best_attn_impl():\n    if importlib.util.find_spec(\"flash_attn\"):\n        return \"flash_attention_2\"\n    return \"sdpa\"\n\n\ndef cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, warmup_steps, total_steps):\n    def lr_lambda(step):\n        if step < warmup_steps:\n            return step / max(1, warmup_steps)\n        progress = (step - warmup_steps) / max(1, total_steps - warmup_steps)\n        return 0.5 * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * progress))\n    return torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LambdaLR(optimizer, lr_lambda)\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef compute_neuron_importance(wrapper, dataloader, device, num_batches=50):\n    \"\"\"Compute per-layer mean |activation| for each intermediate neuron.\"\"\"\n    num_layers = len(wrapper.predictors)\n    accum = [None] * num_layers\n    counts = [0] * num_layers\n\n    wrapper.sparse_mode = False\n    wrapper.capture_intermediates = True\n\n    data_iter = iter(dataloader)\n    for batch_idx in range(num_batches):\n        try:\n            batch = next(data_iter)\n        except StopIteration:\n            data_iter = iter(dataloader)\n            batch = next(data_iter)\n\n        input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n        wrapper._layer_intermediates.clear()\n        wrapper._layer_inputs.clear()\n        wrapper.model(input_ids=input_ids)\n\n        for layer_idx in range(num_layers):\n            if layer_idx not in wrapper._layer_intermediates:\n                continue\n            intermediate = wrapper._layer_intermediates[layer_idx]\n            # mean over batch and seq dims -> [intermediate_size]\n            mag = intermediate.abs().float().mean(dim=(0, 1))\n            if accum[layer_idx] is None:\n                accum[layer_idx] = mag\n            else:\n                accum[layer_idx] += mag\n            counts[layer_idx] += 1\n\n        if (batch_idx + 1) % 10 == 0:\n            print(f\"  Importance computation: {batch_idx+1}/{num_batches} batches\")\n\n    wrapper.sparse_mode = True\n    wrapper.capture_intermediates = False\n\n    importance = {}\n    for i in range(num_layers):\n        if accum[i] is not None and counts[i] > 0:\n            imp = accum[i] / counts[i]\n            # Normalize per layer to [0, 1]\n            imp = imp / (imp.max() + 1e-8)\n            importance[i] = imp.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n    return importance\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef evaluate_ppl_teal_global(wrapper, model, tokenizer, device, sparsity_target=0.5):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate PPL using teal_global_masks protocol (same as paper results).\"\"\"\n    from evaluate import teal_global_masks, evaluate_perplexity, get_eval_dataset as _unused\n    from data_utils import get_eval_dataset\n\n    examples = get_eval_dataset(\"wikitext2\", tokenizer, 2048, max_samples=200)\n    calib_examples = get_eval_dataset(\"wikitext2\", tokenizer, 2048, max_samples=32)\n    calib_ids = torch.stack([ex[\"input_ids\"] for ex in calib_examples]).to(device)\n\n    wrapper.predictors.eval()\n    global_masks = teal_global_masks(wrapper, calib_ids, sparsity_target)\n    ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, examples, device, global_masks=global_masks)\n    wrapper.predictors.train()\n    return ppl\n\n\ndef train_kl_mag_reg(wrapper, dataloader, args, device, neuron_importance):\n    \"\"\"KL training with magnitude-preservation regularization.\"\"\"\n    kl_fn = KLDistillLoss(temperature=args.kl_temperature)\n\n    params = list(wrapper.predictors.parameters())\n    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=args.lr, weight_decay=0.01)\n    scheduler = cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, args.warmup_steps, args.num_steps)\n\n    wrapper.predictors.train()\n    wrapper.model.train()\n    wrapper.model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(\n        gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={\"use_reentrant\": False}\n    )\n    print(\"Gradient checkpointing enabled\")\n\n    data_iter = iter(dataloader)\n    ga = args.gradient_accumulation_steps\n\n    lambda_sparse = 1.0\n    target_sparsity = args.sparsity_target\n    margin = 0.02\n    print(f\"KL + MagReg: alpha={args.alpha}, lambda={lambda_sparse}, target={target_sparsity}, \"\n          f\"margin={margin}, cap={args.lambda_max}\")\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for opt_step in range(1, args.num_steps + 1):\n        tau = args.tau_start + (args.tau_end - args.tau_start) * (opt_step / args.num_steps)\n        wrapper.gumbel_mask.tau = tau\n\n        accum_loss = 0.0\n        accum_kl = 0.0\n        accum_constraint = 0.0\n        accum_mag_reg = 0.0\n\n        for _ in range(ga):\n            try:\n                batch = next(data_iter)\n            except StopIteration:\n                data_iter = iter(dataloader)\n                batch = next(data_iter)\n\n            input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n            dense_logits = wrapper.forward_dense(input_ids).logits.detach()\n            sparse_logits = wrapper.forward_sparse(input_ids).logits\n\n            kl = kl_fn(dense_logits, sparse_logits)\n\n            masks = wrapper.get_masks()\n            actual_sparsity = 1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in masks.values()]).mean()\n\n            # Soft Lagrangian constraint\n            sparsity_error = torch.abs(actual_sparsity - target_sparsity)\n            constraint_loss = lambda_sparse * torch.clamp(sparsity_error - margin, min=0.0) ** 2\n\n            # Magnitude-preservation regularization\n            # Penalize pruning (1-mask) of high-importance neurons\n            mag_reg = torch.tensor(0.0, device=device)\n            for layer_idx, mask in masks.items():\n                if layer_idx in neuron_importance:\n                    imp = neuron_importance[layer_idx]\n                    prune_prob = 1.0 - mask  # [batch, seq, intermediate]\n                    # mean over batch, seq; weighted by importance\n                    layer_penalty = (prune_prob * imp.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)).mean()\n                    mag_reg = mag_reg + layer_penalty\n            mag_reg = mag_reg / len(masks)\n\n            loss = (kl + constraint_loss + args.alpha * mag_reg) / ga\n            loss.backward()\n\n            accum_loss += loss.item()\n            accum_kl += kl.item() / ga\n            accum_constraint += constraint_loss.item() / ga\n            accum_mag_reg += mag_reg.item() / ga\n\n        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)\n        optimizer.step()\n        scheduler.step()\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\n\n        masks = wrapper.get_masks()\n        avg_sparsity = (\n            1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in masks.values()]).mean().item()\n            if masks else 0.0\n        )\n\n        # Lagrangian update (every step, aggressive)\n        old_lambda = lambda_sparse\n        if avg_sparsity < target_sparsity - margin:\n            lambda_sparse *= 2.0\n        elif avg_sparsity > target_sparsity + margin:\n            lambda_sparse *= 0.5\n        lambda_sparse = max(0.01, min(lambda_sparse, args.lambda_max))\n\n        if opt_step % 10 == 0 or opt_step == 1:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(\n                f\"[Step {opt_step}/{args.num_steps}] loss={accum_loss:.6f} \"\n                f\"kl={accum_kl:.4f} constraint={accum_constraint:.6f} \"\n                f\"mag_reg={accum_mag_reg:.4f} sparsity={avg_sparsity:.3f} \"\n                f\"lambda={lambda_sparse:.2f} tau={tau:.3f} \"\n                f\"lr={scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e} elapsed={elapsed:.0f}s\"\n            )\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"Training done in {elapsed:.1f}s ({elapsed/60:.1f}min)\")\n    return avg_sparsity\n\n\ndef main():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"KL + Magnitude-Preservation Regularization\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--model_name_or_path\", type=str, default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_steps\", type=int, default=1000)\n    p.add_argument(\"--batch_size\", type=int, default=4)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seq_len\", type=int, default=2048)\n    p.add_argument(\"--lr\", type=float, default=1e-3)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--sparsity_target\", type=float, default=0.5)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=1)\n    p.add_argument(\"--alpha\", type=float, required=True, help=\"Magnitude reg weight\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str, required=True)\n    p.add_argument(\"--tau_start\", type=float, default=1.0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--tau_end\", type=float, default=0.1)\n    p.add_argument(\"--kl_temperature\", type=float, default=1.0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--warmup_steps\", type=int, default=100)\n    p.add_argument(\"--lambda_max\", type=float, default=1000.0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gradient_accumulation_steps\", type=int, default=1)\n    p.add_argument(\"--bottleneck_dim\", type=int, default=128)\n    p.add_argument(\"--importance_batches\", type=int, default=50)\n    p.add_argument(\"--eval_after\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    p.add_argument(\"--dry_run\", action=\"store_true\")\n    args = p.parse_args()\n\n    device = f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\"\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    print(f\"=== KL + MagReg Training (alpha={args.alpha}) ===\")\n    print(f\"Device: {device}, Steps: {args.num_steps}, Seed: {args.seed}\")\n\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_name_or_path)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        args.model_name_or_path,\n        dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        device_map={\"\": device},\n        attn_implementation=_best_attn_impl(),\n    )\n    model.eval()\n\n    wrapper = PredictorWrapper(model, bottleneck_size=args.bottleneck_dim)\n    wrapper.predictors.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n    dataloader = get_calibration_loader(tokenizer, args.batch_size, args.seq_len, args.seed, \"wikitext-103\")\n\n    trainable = sum(p.numel() for p in wrapper.predictors.parameters())\n    print(f\"Trainable params: {trainable:,}\")\n\n    # Phase 1: Compute neuron importance\n    print(\"\\n--- Phase 1: Computing neuron importance ---\")\n    neuron_importance = compute_neuron_importance(\n        wrapper, dataloader, device, num_batches=args.importance_batches\n    )\n    print(f\"Computed importance for {len(neuron_importance)} layers\")\n    for i in [0, 15, 31]:\n        if i in neuron_importance:\n            imp = neuron_importance[i]\n            print(f\"  Layer {i}: mean={imp.mean():.4f}, max={imp.max():.4f}, min={imp.min():.4f}\")\n\n    if args.dry_run:\n        print(\"Dry run: exiting after importance computation + 2 steps\")\n        args.num_steps = 2\n\n    # Phase 2: Train with magnitude regularization\n    print(f\"\\n--- Phase 2: Training ({args.num_steps} steps) ---\")\n    # Re-create dataloader to reset iterator\n    dataloader = get_calibration_loader(tokenizer, args.batch_size, args.seq_len, args.seed, \"wikitext-103\")\n    final_sparsity = train_kl_mag_reg(wrapper, dataloader, args, device, neuron_importance)\n\n    # Save checkpoint\n    ckpt_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \"predictor_kl_mag_reg.pt\")\n    torch.save({\n        \"predictors\": wrapper.predictors.state_dict(),\n        \"args\": vars(args),\n        \"neuron_importance\": {k: v.cpu() for k, v in neuron_importance.items()},\n    }, ckpt_path)\n    print(f\"Saved checkpoint -> {ckpt_path}\")\n\n    # Phase 3: Evaluate PPL\n    if args.eval_after and not args.dry_run:\n        print(\"\\n--- Phase 3: Evaluating PPL (teal_global protocol) ---\")\n        ppl = evaluate_ppl_teal_global(wrapper, model, tokenizer, device, args.sparsity_target)\n        print(f\"WikiText-2 PPL (teal_global, sparsity={args.sparsity_target}): {ppl:.4f}\")\n\n        results = {\n            \"alpha\": args.alpha,\n            \"ppl\": ppl,\n            \"final_sparsity\": final_sparsity,\n            \"steps\": args.num_steps,\n            \"seed\": args.seed,\n        }\n        results_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \"results.json\")\n        with open(results_path, \"w\") as f:\n            json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n        print(f\"Results saved ->
